## Expert Roles & Specialties

| Expert | Role | Specialties |
|--------|------|------------|
| **torch_expert** | PRIMARY | Deep Learning, CV, NLP, RL, CUDA |
| **tensorflow_expert** | PRIMARY | DL, Serving, TFX, TPU |
| **sklearn_expert** | SECONDARY | Tabular, Clustering, Pipelines |
| **jax_expert** | SPECIALIST | vmap, grad, jit, Research |
| **onnx_expert** | FALLBACK | Export, Optimization, Deployment |
| **ray_expert** | SPECIALIST | Distributed, Tuning, Serving |

## Routing Strategies

### Q-Learning Router
- Learns from experience
- Updates Q-values based on performance
- Balances exploration/exploitation

### Heuristic Router  
- Rule-based fallback
- GPU availability check
- Task-domain matching

### MoE Router
- Combines both strategies
- Confidence-weighted selection
- Ensemble execution support

## Supported Frameworks

| Framework | Format | Notes |
|-----------|--------|-------|
| PyTorch | SafeTensors/.bin | Best support |
| TensorFlow | SavedModel/.pb | Requires weights mapping |
| ONNX | .onnx | Basic export |
| JAX | Config + Weights | NumPy intermediate |

## Model Support

| Model Type | Support | Notes |
|------------|---------|-------|
| LLaMA/Mistral | Full | All quantizations |
| Qwen | Full | VL models need extra step |
| Code Models | Full | NousCoder, etc. |
| Vision Models | Partial | mmproj extraction |

## TIER 2: CLUSTERWORMS
**Coordinated Collectives** - Worm swarms working in unison

### Definition
Groups of worms executing in coordination with shared state and communication protocols.

### Cluster Types

| Cluster | Composition | Use Case |
|---------|-------------|----------|
| **BIBL Cluster** | WORMZ + TRIMZ | Content crawling & trimming |
| **System Cluster** | SYSTEMZ + PROCESSZ + DISKZ | Full system monitoring |
| **Network Cluster** | NETWORXZ + CLOCKZ + DISKZ | Infrastructure health |
| **Security Cluster** | PROCESSZ + NETWORXZ | Security auditing |
| **Analysis Cluster** | METAZ + METRICZ + DEPENDZ | Code analysis |
| **Validation Cluster** | VALIDZ + DEADZ + ERRORZ | Code validation |
| **Health Cluster** | HEALTHZ + OPTIMIZ + SYSTEMZ | System health |
| **Full Cluster** | SYSTEMZ + PROCESSZ + DISKZ + NETWORXZ + WORMZ | Complete ecosystem |

## ray_integration.py

**Solves:** No distributed computing (27/27 platforms)

**Problem:** No unified parallel/distributed API

**Solution:** Ray-based distributed computing wrapper

** - Term: 

**Key Features:**
- Auto-detects model framework (PyTorch, sklearn, XGBo...
- **

**Key Features:**
- Broadcasting-based vectorization
- JIT function caching
- Numerical gradient computation

---

## onnx_integration.py

**Solves:** ONNX export unstable (27/27 platforms)

**Problem:** No unified ONNX export across frameworks

**Solution:** Framework-aware ONNX export/import

** - Term: 

**Key Features:**
- Broadcasting-based vectorization
- JIT function cach...
- **

**Key Features:**
- Decorator-based remote execution
- Automatic resource allocation
- Distributed training wrapper

## vectorize_utils.py

**Solves:** No vectorization (27/27 platforms)

**Solution:** JAX-style vmap, jit, grad utilities

** - Term:

- Decorator-based remote execution

**Key Features:**
- Lazy imports (no install required)
- Device-aware conversion (CPU/GPU)
- Round-trip data preservation

### Q-Value

**Category:** Q-Learning

**Definition:** Expected cumulative reward for taking action a in state s and following the optimal policy thereafter.

**Also Known As:** Action Value

**See Also:**
- [Q-Table](M-R.md#q-table)
- [Bellman Equation](M-R.md#bellman-equation)

6. Feedback - Generate feedback report

**Features:**
- Dependency-aware processing
- Adaptive learning
- Priority scheduling
- Retry logic
- Feedback generation

## HYDRA Cluster with SHORTY Integration

### 6-Stage Processing

| Stage | SHORTY Protocol | Description |
|-------|-----------------|-------------|
| DISCOVERY | INDEX | Index directories via SHORTY |
| ANALYSIS | SEARCH | Analyze indexed content |
| OPTIMIZE | VALIDATE | Optimize based on file metrics |
| VALIDATE | VALIDATE | Validate files via SHORTY |
| DEPLOY | SYNC | Deploy using sync protocol |
| FEEDBACK | READ | Collect execution feedback |

## Routing Strategies

### Q-Learning Router
- Learns from experience
- Updates Q-values based on performance
- Balances exploration/exploitation (epsilon-greedy)

### Heuristic Router (Fallback)
- Rule-based selection
- Checks GPU availability
- Matches task to domain

### MoE Router (Combined)
- Uses Q-learning when confident
- Falls back to heuristics when uncertain
- Supports ensemble execution

---

| 6.0.0 | 2026-02-11 | Added ML worms (WORMZ_ML, SYSTEMZ_ML, SMARTDISK), Telemetry (METRICZ, TELEMETRYZ), UV integration (UVZ, HYDRAMETA_UV), Containerization (DOCKERZ, HYDRA_CONTAINER), SHORTY shortcuts |
| 5.0.0 | 2026-02-11 | Added Azazael_INTEGRATION, SHORTY filesystem protocol, HYDRA cluster integration |
| 4.0.0 | 2026-02-11 | Added OS_CONGRUENCY, CONCURRENCY, 7-tier architecture |
| 3.0.0 | 2026-02-11 | Added HYDRAMETA, clusterworms, metaworms expansion |
| 2.0.0 | 2026-02-11 | Added SYSTEMZ, PROCESSZ, DISKZ, NETWORXZ, CLOCKZ |
| 1.0.0 | 2026-02-11 | Initial WORMZ + TERENCE |

## Azazael_INTEGRATION - Comprehensive Ecosystem Integration

### Features

| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| Unified Component Registry | Auto-discovers all ecosystem components |
| Cross-Component Communication | Seamless communication between all modules |
| HYDRA Cluster Integration | 6-stage sequential processing |
| TERENCE Error Resolution | Built-in error handling propagation |
| HORUS Output Formatting | Human-readable results via HORUS |
| SHORTY Shortcut Execution | Execute .bat shortcuts from SHORTY folder |
| Cotemporaneous Execution | Parallel operations via ThreadPoolExecutor |

## Tier 0: CONCURRENCY - Cotemporaneous Execution

### CONCURRENCY.py Features

| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| Thread Pool | Configurable workers (default: 8) |
| Parallel Worm Execution | SYSTEMZ, PROCESSZ, DISKZ, NETWORXZ concurrently |
| Parallel Cluster Execution | Multiple clusters at once |
| Thread-Safe Operations | Lock-protected shared state |
| Task Manager | Submit/track tasks |
| Worm Executor | Load and execute worms concurrently |
| Cluster Executor | Execute clusters in parallel |

## Tier 2: CLUSTERWORMS - Coordinated Collectives

### Cluster Types

| Cluster | Worms | Use Case |
|---------|-------|----------|
| **BIBL** | WORMZ + TRIMZ | Content crawling & trimming |
| **System** | SYSTEMZ + PROCESSZ + DISKZ | Full system monitoring |
| **Network** | NETWORXZ + CLOCKZ + DISKZ | Infrastructure health |
| **Security** | PROCESSZ + NETWORXZ + SYSTEMZ | Security auditing |
| **Analysis** | METAZ + METRICZ + DEPENDZ | Code analysis |
| **Validation** | VALIDZ + DEADZ + ERRORZ | Code validation |
| **Health** | HEALTHZ + OPTIMIZ + SYSTEMZ | System health |
| **Full** | SYSTEMZ + PROCESSZ + DISKZ + NETWORXZ + WORMZ | Complete ecosystem |

### run_qanalysis_multi.py

**Purpose:** Multi-iteration Q-Learning (10 iterations)

**What it does:**
- Runs analysis 10 times with incremental learning
- Tracks convergence over iterations
- Shows improvement from initial to final Q-values

---

### run_qanalysis_full.py

**Purpose:** Full convergence Q-Learning (100 iterations)

**What it does:**
- Runs 100 iterations for complete convergence
- Uses decaying learning rate (alpha decay)
- Outputs final converged Q-table and recommendations

│   │   • PyTorch      → mlflow.pytorch.log_model()       │   │
│   │   • TensorFlow   → mlflow.tensorflow.log_model()    │   │
│   │   • Keras        → mlflow.keras.log_model()          │   │
│   │   • sklearn      → mlflow.sklearn.log_model()       │   │
│   │   • XGBoost      → mlflow.xgboost.log_model()      │   │
│   │   • LightGBM     → mlflow.lightgbm.log_model()     │   │
│   │   • CatBoost     → mlflow.catboost.log_model()     │   │
│   │   • Custom       → mlflow.pyfunc.log_model()       │   │
│   └───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │
│                                                                 │
│   Features:                                                     │
│   • Framework auto-detection                                     │

## S

### Sequential Protocol

**Category:** Cluster System

**Definition:** Successive processing stages used by HYDRA for ordered ML module development.

**Stages:**
| Stage | Purpose | Dependencies |
|-------|---------|--------------|
| Discovery | Auto-detect modules | None |
| Analysis | Process Analysis group | Discovery |
| Optimization | Optimize Q-Learning | Analysis |
| Validation | Validate Core/Monitoring | Optimization |
| Deployment | Deploy LM Studio | Validation |
| Feedback | Generate report | All complete |

**See Also:**
- [HYDRA](G-L.md#hydra)
- [Processing Stages](A-F.md#processing-stages)

---

- You're exploring interactively

- You're debugging and want a live environment

- **python
# PyTorch updates
q_table[0, 0] += alpha * (torch_score - q_table[0, 0])   # CV
q_table[1, 0] += alpha * (0.90 - q_table[1, 0])        # NLP
q_table[3, 0] += alpha * (0.95 - q_table[3, 0])        # RL

# TensorFlow updates
q_table[0, 1] += alpha * (tf_score - q_table[0, 1])   # CV
q_table[1, 1] += alpha * (0.88 - q_table[1, 1])      # NLP

# JAX updates
q_table[4, 3] += alpha * (0.92 - q_table[4, 3])       # Generative
q_table[3, 3] += alpha * (0.88 - q_table[3, 3])       # RL

# SciPy updates
q_table[2, 2] += alpha * (0.95 - q_table[2, 2])       # Tabular
q_table[2, 4] += alpha * (0.85 - q_table[2, 4])       # Tabular (scipy)

- **python
@staticmethod
def detect_framework(model) -> Optional[str]:
    """Detect model framework from type."""
    
    model_type = type(model).__module__
    
    # Check PyTorch
    if hasattr(model, "state_dict"):
        return "pytorch"
    
    # Check TensorFlow/Keras
    if hasattr(model, "variables") or hasattr(model, "get_config"):
        return "tensorflow"
    
    # Check sklearn
    if "sklearn" in model_type:
        return "sklearn"
    
    # Check XGBoost
    if "xgboost" in model_type:
        return "xgboost"
    
    # Check LightGBM
    if "lightgbm" in model_type:
        return "lightgbm"
    
    return None
** - Term: python

class MLflowLogger:
    """
    Universal MLflow logger that works with any ML framework.
    
    Why this matters:
    - 27 platforms lack MLflow tracking integration
    - Each framework has different log_model API
    - MLOps requires consistent experiment tracking
    """
    
    def __init__(
        self,
        experiment_name: str,
        tracking_uri: Optional[str] = None,
        artifact_location: Optional[str] = None,
    ):
        """Initialize logger."""
        
    def start_run(self, run_name: Optional[str] = None) -> "MLflowLogger":
        """Start an MLflow run."""
        
    def end_run(self):
        """End the current MLflow run."""
        
    def log_params(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Log parameters."""
        
    def log_metrics(
        self,
        metrics: Dict[str, float],
        step: Optional[int] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Log metrics."""
        
    def log_model(
        self,
        model: Any,
        artifact_path: str,
        framework: Optional[str] = None,
        registered_model_name: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Log model from any framework."""
        
    def log_artifact(self, local_path: str) -> None:
        """Log a local file as artifact."""
        
    def log_dict(self, dictionary: Dict, artifact_file: str) -> None:
        """Log dict as JSON/YAML artifact."""
        
    def set_tag(self, key: str, value: str) -> None:
        """Set tag on current run."""

class ONNXExporter:
    """
    Unified ONNX export for any ML framework.
    
    Why this matters:
    - 27 platforms lack stable ONNX export
    - ONNX is critical for model interoperability
    - Deployment requires ONNX in production
    """
    
    @staticmethod
    def detect_framework(model) -> Optional[str]:
        """Detect model framework from type."""
        
    @staticmethod
    def export_to_onnx(
        model,
        input_sample,
        output_path,
        input_names=None,
        output_names=None,
        dynamic_axes=None,
        opset_version=13,
    ) -> str:
        """Export model to ONNX format."""
        
    @staticmethod
    def load_onnx_model(
        model_path,
        providers=("CPUExecutionProvider",),
    ) -> ort.InferenceSession:
        """Load ONNX model for inference."""
        
    @staticmethod
    def run_inference(
        session,
        inputs,
        output_names=None,
    ) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, ...]:
        """Run inference on ONNX model."""
        
    @staticmethod
    def optimize_onnx(
        input_path,
        output_path,
        level=1,
    ) -> str:
        """Optimize ONNX model."""

    - Distributed training requires complex setup

        self,

        num_gpus: float = 0.0,

    ):

        self,
        func: Callable,
        items: List[Any],
        **ray_kwargs,
    ) -> List[Any]:
        """Apply function in parallel to list."""
        
    def distribute_data(
        self,
        data: List[Any],
        num_partitions: int,
    ) -> List[Any]:
        """Distribute data across cluster."""
        
    def create_actor(self, cls, *args, **kwargs):
        """Create a Ray actor."""

- **python
def _detect_framework(self, model: Any) -> str:
    """Auto-detect model framework from type."""
    
    model_type = type(model).__module__.split(".")[0]
    
    framework_map = {
        "sklearn": ["sklearn", "sklearn"],
        "torch": ["torch", "pytorch"],
        "tensorflow": ["tensorflow", "tf"],
        "keras": ["keras"],
        "xgboost": ["xgboost"],
        "lightgbm": ["lightgbm"],
        "catboost": ["catboost"],
    }
    
    for framework, prefixes in framework_map.items():
        if any(model_type.startswith(p) for p in prefixes):
            return framework
    
    return "generic"

    batch_size = config["batch_size"]
    # Train model
    return accuracy

    {"lr": 0.001, "batch_size": 32},
    {"lr": 0.01, "batch_size": 32},
    {"lr": 0.001, "batch_size": 64},
]

# Run trials in parallel

import importlib

def get_version(pkg_name):
    try:
        mod = importlib.import_module(pkg_name.replace("-", "_"))
        return getattr(mod, "__version__", "unknown")
    except ImportError:
        return None

import torch
from improvements.onnx_integration import export_to_onnx

# Define model
class MyModel(torch.nn.Module):
    def forward(self, x):
        return x @ torch.tensor([[1.0, 2.0], [3.0, 4.0]]) + 0.5

model = MyModel()
input_sample = torch.randn(1, 2)

# Export

    model,
    input_sample,
    "my_model.onnx",
    input_names=("input",),
    output_names=("output",),
    dynamic_axes={0: "batch_size", 1: "sequence_length"},
    opset_version=13,
)

print("Model exported!")
